{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_01KWgFE3xeRZ7FxZhFMvpaLm","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_012hKbQ85H9HV1yemjRMPgrL","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/Tokenizer.scala","content":"/**\n  * Tokenizer module for converting various input types to standardized string tokens.\n  *\n  * This Scala implementation translates Python's flexible type system into\n  * idiomatic Scala with:\n  * - Sealed trait hierarchies for type safety\n  * - Generic type parameters with variance annotations\n  * - Immutable data structures by default\n  * - Pattern matching for type dispatch\n  * - Option[T] for safe handling of absence\n  */\n\npackage tokenizer\n\nimport java.time.{LocalDate, LocalDateTime, ZonedDateTime}\nimport java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter\nimport scala.collection.mutable\nimport scala.math.BigDecimal\nimport scala.util.{Try, Success, Failure}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Token Type Sealed Hierarchy\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Sealed trait representing the type of a token.\n  * Replaces Python's TokenType enum with Scala's algebraic data type pattern.\n  */\nsealed trait TokenType {\n  def value: String\n}\n\ncase object STRING extends TokenType {\n  val value = \"string\"\n}\n\ncase object NUMERIC extends TokenType {\n  val value = \"numeric\"\n}\n\ncase object TEMPORAL extends TokenType {\n  val value = \"temporal\"\n}\n\ncase object STRUCTURED extends TokenType {\n  val value = \"structured\"\n}\n\ncase object BINARY extends TokenType {\n  val value = \"binary\"\n}\n\ncase object NULL extends TokenType {\n  val value = \"null\"\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Protocol Traits (Structural Typing)\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Trait for any object that can be converted to a token string.\n  */\ntrait Tokenizable {\n  def toToken: String\n}\n\n/**\n  * Trait for any object with a length.\n  */\ntrait HasLength {\n  def length: Int\n}\n\n/**\n  * Trait for processing tokens.\n  * Contravariant type parameter [-T] allows accepting supertypes.\n  */\ntrait TokenProcessor[-T] {\n  def process(item: T): Unit\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Core Token Classes\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Immutable token representation.\n  *\n  * @param value The token string value\n  * @param tokenType The type classification of this token\n  * @param metadata Additional metadata about the token\n  */\ncase class Token(\n    value: String,\n    tokenType: TokenType,\n    metadata: Map[String, Any] = Map.empty\n) {\n  /**\n    * Return a new token with additional metadata.\n    * Preserves immutability by creating a new instance.\n    */\n  def withMetadata(newMetadata: (String, Any)*): Token = {\n    val combined = metadata ++ newMetadata.toMap\n    Token(value, tokenType, combined)\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Mutable batch of tokens for accumulation before processing.\n  * Contrast with immutable Token class.\n  */\ncase class MutableTokenBatch(\n    tokens: mutable.Buffer[Token] = mutable.Buffer.empty,\n    private var processed: Boolean = false\n) {\n  /**\n    * Add a token to the batch.\n    *\n    * @throws RuntimeException if batch has already been marked as processed\n    */\n  def add(token: Token): Unit = {\n    if (processed) throw new RuntimeException(\"Batch already processed\")\n    tokens += token\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Mark this batch as processed, preventing further additions.\n    */\n  def markProcessed(): Unit = {\n    processed = true\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Generic Container Classes\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Covariant container for tokens.\n  * The covariant type parameter [+T] allows returning subtypes.\n  *\n  * @param items The sequence of items to store\n  * @tparam T The item type (covariant)\n  */\nclass TokenContainer[+T](items: Seq[T]) {\n  private val _items = items.toVector\n\n  /**\n    * Get all items in the container.\n    */\n  def getAll: Vector[T] = _items\n\n  /**\n    * Map a function over all tokens to produce strings.\n    */\n  def mapTokens[S](func: T => String): List[String] = {\n    _items.map(func).toList\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Contravariant sink for consuming tokens.\n  * The contravariant type parameter [-T] allows accepting supertypes.\n  *\n  * @tparam T The item type (contravariant)\n  */\nclass TokenSink[-T] {\n  private val _received = mutable.Buffer.empty[Any]\n\n  /**\n    * Receive an item into the sink.\n    */\n  def receive(item: T): Unit = {\n    _received += item\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Drain all received items and clear the buffer.\n    */\n  def drain(): List[Any] = {\n    val result = _received.toList\n    _received.clear()\n    result\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Invariant handler for getting and setting values.\n  * Requires =>\n      Token(\"NULL\", NULL)\n\n    case tokenizable: Tokenizable =>\n      Token(tokenizable.toToken, STRUCTURED)\n\n    case str: String =>\n      stringTokenizer.tokenize(str)\n\n    case bytes: Array[Byte] =>\n      stringTokenizer.tokenize(bytes)\n\n    case i: Int | l: Long =>\n      numericTokenizer.tokenize(i)\n\n    case f: Float | d: Double =>\n      numericTokenizer.tokenize(f)\n\n    case bd: BigDecimal =>\n      numericTokenizer.tokenize(bd)\n\n    case ldt: LocalDateTime | zdt: ZonedDateTime | ld: LocalDate =>\n      temporalTokenizer.tokenize(value)\n\n    case _ =>\n      // Fallback: try string conversion\n      Token(value.toString, STRING, Map(\"fallback\" -> true))\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Basic tokenizer that splits text by whitespace.\n  * Fundamental operation used in NLP and text processing.\n  *\n  * @param lowercase Convert all tokens to lowercase\n  * @param minLength Minimum token length (shorter tokens filtered out)\n  * @param maxLength Optional maximum token length (longer tokens truncated)\n  * @param stripPunctuation Remove leading/trailing punctuation from tokens\n  */\nclass WhitespaceTokenizer(\n    lowercase: Boolean = false,\n    minLength: Int = 0,\n    maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n    stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n  private val punctuation = Set(\".,!?;:'\\\"()[]{}\")\n\n  /**\n    * Process a single word into a token string.\n    * Returns None if the word is filtered out.\n    */\n  private def processToken(word: String): Option[String] = {\n    var processed = word\n\n    if (stripPunctuation) {\n      processed = processed\n        .dropWhile(punctuation.contains)\n        .reverse\n        .dropWhile(punctuation.contains)\n        .reverse\n    }\n\n    if (lowercase) {\n      processed = processed.toLowerCase\n    }\n\n    if (processed.length < minLength) {\n      return None\n    }\n\n    processed = maxLength\n      .map(max => processed.take(max))\n      .getOrElse(processed)\n\n    if (processed.isEmpty) None else Some(processed)\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Split text by whitespace and return list of tokens.\n    */\n  def tokenize(text: String): List[Token] = {\n    val words = text.split(\"\\\\s+\").toList\n    words.zipWithIndex\n      .flatMap { case (word, idx) =>\n        processToken(word).map { processed =>\n          Token(\n            value = processed,\n            tokenType = STRING,\n            metadata = Map(\"position\" -> idx, \"original\" -> word)\n          )\n        }\n      }\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Split text by whitespace and return list of token strings.\n    * Convenience method returning just the string values.\n    */\n  def tokenizeToStrings(text: String): List[String] = {\n    tokenize(text).map(_.value)\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Tokenize text and return tokens with character positions.\n    * Returns tuples of (token_string, start_pos, end_pos).\n    */\n  def tokenizeWithPositions(text: String): List[(String, Int, Int)] = {\n    var currentPos = 0\n    val words = text.split(\"\\\\s+\").toList\n\n    words.flatMap { word =>\n      val start = text.indexOf(word, currentPos)\n      val end = start + word.length\n\n      processToken(word).map { processed =>\n        currentPos = end\n        (processed, start, end)\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Return the number of tokens in the text.\n    */\n  def countTokens(text: String): Int = {\n    tokenize(text).length\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Tokenizer for JSON structures with recursive types.\n  *\n  * @param pretty Pretty-print JSON with indentation\n  */\nclass JsonTokenizer(pretty: Boolean = false) {\n  /**\n    * Tokenize a JSON value.\n    */\n  def tokenize(value: JsonValue): Token = {\n    val jsonStr = jsonValueToString(value, pretty)\n    Token(jsonStr, STRUCTURED, Map(\"json\" -> true))\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Extract and tokenize a value at a JSON path.\n    * Path format: \"key1.key2.0.key3\" for nested access.\n    */\n  def tokenizePath(value: JsonValue, path: String): Option[Token] = {\n    val parts = path.split(\"\\\\.\")\n    traversePath(value, parts).map(tokenize)\n  }\n\n  private def traversePath(current: JsonValue, parts: Seq[String]): Option[JsonValue] = {\n    if (parts.isEmpty) return Some(current)\n\n    val (head, tail) = (parts.head, parts.tail)\n\n    current match {\n      case obj: JsonObject =>\n        obj.get(head).flatMap(traversePath(_, tail))\n\n      case arr: JsonArray =>\n        Try(head.toInt).toOption.flatMap { idx =>\n          if (idx >= 0 && idx < arr.length) {\n            traversePath(arr(idx), tail)\n          } else {\n            None\n          }\n        }\n\n      case _ => None\n    }\n  }\n\n  private def jsonValueToString(value: JsonValue, pretty: Boolean): String = {\n    value match {\n      case null => \"null\"\n      case str: String => s\"\\\"$str\\\"\"\n      case num: (Int | Long | Double | Float) => num.toString\n      case bool: Boolean => bool.toString\n      case arr: JsonArray =>\n        if (pretty) {\n          arr.map(jsonValueToString(_, pretty)).mkString(\"[\\n  \", \",\\n  \", \"\\n]\")\n        } else {\n          arr.map(jsonValueToString(_, pretty)).mkString(\"[\", \",\", \"]\")\n        }\n      case obj: JsonObject =>\n        if (pretty) {\n          obj.map { case (k, v) =>\n            s\"\\\"$k\\\": ${jsonValueToString(v, pretty)}\"\n          }.mkString(\"{\\n  \", \",\\n  \", \"\\n}\")\n        } else {\n          obj.map { case (k, v) =>\n            s\"\\\"$k\\\":${jsonValueToString(v, pretty)}\"\n          }.mkString(\"{\", \",\", \"}\")\n        }\n      case _ => value.toString\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Type aliases for JSON values.\n  * Replaces Python's recursive Union type with Scala sealed hierarchy.\n  */\ntype JsonObject = Map[String, JsonValue]\ntype JsonArray = Vector[JsonValue]\n\nsealed trait JsonValue\ncase object JsonNull extends JsonValue\nfinal case class JsonString(value: String) extends JsonValue\nfinal case class JsonNumber(value: Double) extends JsonValue\nfinal case class JsonBoolean(value: Boolean) extends JsonValue\nfinal case class JsonArr(value: Vector[JsonValue]) extends JsonValue\nfinal case class JsonObj(value: Map[String, JsonValue]) extends JsonValue\n\n//  * Registry with complex nested generic types.\n  * Demonstrates mixing mutable and immutable collections with generics.\n  *\n  * @tparam T The item type stored in containers\n  */\nclass TokenRegistry[T] {\n  private val _registry = mutable.Map.empty[String, TokenContainer[T]]\n  private val _handlers = mutable.Buffer.empty[T => Option[Token]]\n\n  /**\n    * Register a container under a key.\n    */\n  def register(key: String, container: TokenContainer[T]): Unit = {\n    _registry(key) = container\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Add a handler function.\n    */\n  def addHandler(handler: T => Option[Token]): Unit = {\n    _handlers += handler\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Process all items in a container through all handlers.\n    * Returns results from the first successful handler for each item.\n    */\n  def process(key: String): List[Option[Token]] = {\n    _registry.get(key) match {\n      case None => List()\n      case Some(container) =>\n        container.getAll.map { item =>\n          _handlers.iterator\n            .flatMap { handler =>\n              handler(item).toIterator\n            }\n            .nextOption()\n        }.toList\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Functor and Monad Abstractions\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Functor abstraction for tokens.\n  * Simulates higher-kinded types that Python cannot express.\n  *\n  * @param value The wrapped value\n  * @tparam T The value type\n  */\nclass TokenFunctor[T](val value: T) {\n  /**\n    * Map a function over the wrapped value.\n    */\n  def map[B](func: T => B): TokenFunctor[B] = {\n    func(value)\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Get the wrapped value or use a default if None.\n    */\n  def getOrElse(default: T): T = {\n    if (value == null) default else value\n applies all configured transformations.\n    */\n  def build: T => Token = {\n    val normalizers = _normalizers\n    val validators = _validators\n    val metadata = _metadata\n\n    (value: T) =>  * @param encoding The character encoding for bytes (default: UTF-8)\n  * @param normalizer Optional function to normalize strings after decoding\n  */\nclass StringTokenizer(\n    encoding: String = \"UTF-8\",\n    normalizer: String => String = identity\n) extends BaseTokenizer[Any] {\n  def tokenize(value: Any): Token = {\n    val strValue = value match {\n      case bytes: Array[Byte] =>\n        new String(bytes, encoding)\n      case bytes: Seq[Byte] =>\n        new String(bytes.toArray, encoding)\n      case str: String =>\n        str\n      case other =>\n        other.toString\n    }\n    Token(normalizer(strValue), STRING)\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Tokenizer for numeric types with precision handling.\n  *\n  * @param precision Number of decimal places for formatting (default: 6)\n  * @param formatOptions Additional formatting options\n  */\nclass NumericTokenizer(\n    precision: Int = 6,\n    formatOptions: Map[String, Any] = Map.empty\n) extends BaseTokenizer[Any] {\n  def tokenize(value: Any): Token = {\n    val strValue = value match {\n      case bd: BigDecimal =>\n        f\"${bd.toDouble}%.${precision}f\"\n      case d: Double =>\n        f\"$d%.${precision}f\"\n      case f: Float =>\n        val dval = f.toDouble\n        f\"$dval%.${precision}f\"\n      case i: Int =>\n        i.toString\n      case l: Long =>\n        l.toString\n      case other =>\n formatStr\n      .map(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern(_))\n      .getOrElse {\n        value match {\n          case _: LocalDateTime => isoFormatter\n          case _: ZonedDateTime => isoFormatter\n          case _: LocalDate => dateFormatter\n          case _ => isoFormatter\n        }\n      }\n\n    val strValue = value match {\n      case ldt: LocalDateTime => ldt.format(formatter)\n      case stringTokenizer = new StringTokenizer()\n  private val numericTokenizer = new NumericTokenizer()\n  private val temporalTokenizer = new TemporalTokenizer()\n\n  /**\n    * Dispatch to appropriate tokenizer based on runtime type.\n    * Uses pattern matching for type-safe dispatch that replaces Python's isinstance checks.\n    */\n  def tokenize(value: Any): Token = value match {\n    case null =>\n      Token(\"NULL\", NULL)\n\n    case tokenizable: Tokenizable =>\n      Token(tokenizable.toToken, STRUCTURED)\n\n    case str: String =>\n      stringTokenizer.tokenize(str)\n\n    case bytes: Array[Byte] =>\n      stringTokenizer.tokenize(bytes)\n\n    case i: Int | l: Long =>\n      numericTokenizer.tokenize(i)\n\n    case f: Float | d: Double =>\n      numericTokenizer.tokenize(f)\n\n    case bd: BigDecimal =>\n      numericTokenizer.tokenize(bd)\n\n    case ldt: LocalDateTime | zdt: ZonedDateTime | ld: LocalDate =>\n      temporalTokenizer.tokenize(value)\n\n    case _ =>\n      // NLP and text processing.\n  *\n  * @param lowercase Convert all tokens to lowercase\n  * @param minLength Minimum token length (shorter tokens filtered out)\n  * @param maxLength Optional maximum token length (longer tokens truncated)\n  * @param stripPunctuation Remove leading/trailing punctuation from tokens\n  */\nclass WhitespaceTokenizer(\n    lowercase: Boolean = false,\n    minLength: Int = 0,\n    maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n    stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n  private val punctuation = Set(\".,!?;:'\\\"()[]{}\")\n\n  /**\n    * Process a single word into a token string.\n    * Returns None if the word is filtered out.\n    */\n  private def processToken(word: String): Option[String] = {\n    var processed = word\n\n    if (stripPunctuation) {\n      processed = processed\n        .dropWhile(punctuation.contains)\n        .reverse\n        .dropWhile(punctuation.contains)\n        .reverse\n    }\n\n    if (lowercase) {\n      processed = processed.toLowerCase\n    }\n\n    if (processed.length < minLength) {\n      return None\n    }\n\n    processed = maxLength\n      .map(max => processed.take(max))\n      .getOrElse(processed)\n\n    if (processed.isEmpty) None else Some(processed)\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Split text by whitespace and return list of tokens.\n    */\n  def tokenize(text: String): List[Token] = {\n    val words = text.split(\"\\\\s+\").toList\n    words.zipWithIndex\n      .flatMap { case (word, idx) =>\n        processToken(word).map { processed =>\n          Token(\n            value = processed,\n            tokenType = STRING,\n            metadata = Map(\"position\" -> idx, \"original\" -> word)\n          )\n        }\n      }\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Split text by whitespace and return list of token strings.\n    * Convenience method returning just the string values.\n    */\n  def tokenizeToStrings(text: String): List[String] = {\n    tokenize(text).map(_.value)\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Tokenize text and return tokens with character positions.\n    * Returns tuples of (token_string, start_pos, end_pos).\n    */\n  def tokenizeWithPositions(text: String): List[(String, Int, Int)] = {\n    var currentPos = 0\n    val words = text.split(\"\\\\s+\").toList\n\n    words.flatMap { word =>\n      val start = text.indexOf(word, currentPos)\n      val end = start + word.length\n\n      processToken(word).map { processed =>\n        currentPos = end\n        (processed, start, end)\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Return the number of tokens in the text.\n    */\n  def countTokens(text: String): Int = {\n    tokenize(text).length\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Tokenizer for JSON structures with recursive types.\n  *\n  * @param pretty Pretty-print JSON with indentation\n  */\nclass JsonTokenizer(pretty: Boolean = false) {\n  /**\n    * Tokenize a JSON value.\n    */\n  def tokenize(value: JsonValue): Token = {\n    val jsonStr = jsonValueToString(value, pretty)\n    Token(jsonStr, STRUCTURED, Map(\"json\" -> true))\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Extract and tokenize a value at a JSON path.\n    * Path format: \"key1.key2.0.key3\" for nested access.\n    */\n  def tokenizePath(value: JsonValue, path: String): Option[Token] = {\n    val parts = path.split(\"\\\\.\")\n    traversePath(value, parts).map(tokenize)\n  }\n\n  private def traversePath(current: JsonValue, parts: Seq[String]): Option[JsonValue] = {\n    if (parts.isEmpty) return Some(current)\n\n    val (head, tail) = (parts.head, parts.tail)\n\n    current match {\n      case obj: JsonObject =>\n        obj.get(head).flatMap(traversePath(_, tail))\n\n      case arr: JsonArray =>\n        Try(head.toInt).toOption.flatMap { idx =>\n          if (idx >= 0 && idx < arr.length) {\n            traversePath(arr(idx), tail)\n          } else {\n            None\n          }\n        }\n\n      case _ => None\n    }\n  }\n\n  private def jsonValueToString(value: JsonValue, pretty: Boolean): String = {\n    value match {\n      case null => \"null\"\n      case str: String => s\"\\\"$str\\\"\"\n      case num: (Int | Long | Double | Float) => num.toString\n      case bool: Boolean => bool.toString\n      case arr: JsonArray =>\n        if (pretty) {\n          arr.map(jsonValueToString(_, pretty)).mkString(\"[\\n  \", \",\\n  \", \"\\n]\")\n        } else {\n          arr.map(jsonValueToString(_, pretty)).mkString(\"[\", \",\", \"]\")\n        }\n      case obj: JsonObject =>\n        if (pretty) {\n          obj.map { case (k, v) =>\n            s\"\\\"$k\\\": ${jsonValueToString(v, pretty)}\"\n          }.mkString(\"{\\n  \", \",\\n  \", \"\\n}\")\n        } else {\n          obj.map { case (k, v) =>\n            s\"\\\"$k\\\":${jsonValueToString(v, pretty)}\"\n          }.mkString(\"{\", \",\", \"}\")\n        }\n      case _ => value.toString\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Sealed trait for JSON values.\n  * Replaces Python's recursive Union type with Scala algebraic data type.\n  */\nsealed trait JsonValue\ncase object JsonNull extends JsonValue\nfinal case class JsonString(value: String) extends JsonValue\nfinal case class JsonNumber(value: Double) extends JsonValue\nfinal case class JsonBoolean(value: Boolean) extends JsonValue\nfinal case class JsonArr(value: Vector[JsonValue]) extends JsonValue\nfinal case class JsonObj(value: Map[String, JsonValue]) extends JsonValue\n\n// Type aliases for JSON values\ntype JsonObject = Map[String, JsonValue]\ntype JsonArray = Vector[JsonValue]\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Registry with Nested Generics\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Registry with complex nested generic types.\n  * Demonstrates mixing mutable and immutable collections with generics.\n  *\n  * @tparam T The item type stored in containers\n  */\nclass TokenRegistry[T] {\n  private val _registry = mutable.Map.empty[String, TokenContainer[T]]\n  private val _handlers = mutable.Buffer.empty[T => Option[Token]]\n\n  /**\n    * Register a container under a key.\n    */\n  def register(key: String, container: TokenContainer[T]): Unit = {\n    _registry(key) = container\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Add a handler function.\n    */\n  def addHandler(handler: T => Option[Token]): Unit = {\n    _handlers += handler\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Process all items in a container through all handlers.\n    * Returns results from the first successful handler for each item.\n    */\n  def process(key: String): List[Option[Token]] = {\n    _registry.get(key) match {\n      case None => List()\n      case Some(container) =>\n        container.getAll.map { item =>\n          _handlers.iterator\n            .flatMap { handler =>\n              handler(item).toIterator\n            }\n            .nextOption()\n        }.toList\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Functor and Monad Abstractions\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Functor abstraction for tokens.\n  * Simulates higher-kinded types that Python cannot express.\n  *\n  * @param value The wrapped value\n  * @tparam T The value type\n  */\nclass TokenFunctor[T](val value: T) {\n  /**\n    * Map a function over the wrapped value.\n    */\n  def map[B](func: T => B): TokenFunctor[B] = {\n    new TokenFunctor(func(value))\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Flatmap (monadic bind) a function over the wrapped value.\n    */\n  def flatMap[B](func: T => TokenFunctor[B]): TokenFunctor[B] = {\n    func(value)\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Get the wrapped value or use a default if None.\n    */\n  def getOrElse(default: T): T = {\n    if (value == null) default else value\n {\n  /**\n    * Apply a wrapped function to this monad.\n    */\n  def ap[B](funcWrapped: TokenMonad[T => B]): TokenMonad[B] = {\n    new TokenMonad(funcWrapped.value(value))\n  }\n}\n\nobject ============================================================================\n// Builder Pattern with Fluent Interface\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Fluent builder for creating custom tokenizers.\n  * Demonstrates method chaining with generic return types in Scala.\n  *\n  * @tparam T The input type\n  */\nclass TokenizerBuilder[T] {\n  private var _normalizers: List[String => String] = List()\n  private var _validators: List[T => Boolean] = List()\n =>\n      Token(\"NULL\", NULL)\n\n    case tokenizable: Tokenizable =>\n      Token(tokenizable.toToken, STRUCTURED)\n\n    case str: String =>\n      stringTokenizer.tokenize(str)\n\n    case bytes: token length (longer tokens truncated)\n  * @param stripPunctuation Remove leading/trailing punctuation from tokens\n  */\nclass WhitespaceTokenizer(\n    lowercase: Boolean = false,\n    minLength: processed.toLowerCase\n    }\n\n    if (processed.length < minLength) {\n      return None\n    }\n\n    processed = maxLength\n      .map(max => processed.take(max))\n      .getOrElse(processed)\n\n text.split(\"\\\\s+\").toList\n    words.zipWithIndex\n      .flatMap { case (word, idx) =>\n        processToken(word).map { processed =>\n          Token(\n            value = processed,\n text and return tokens with character positions.\n    * Returns tuples of (token_string, start_pos, end_pos).\n    */\n  def tokenizeWithPositions(text: String): List[(String, Int, Int)] = {\n    var currentPos = 0\n    val words = text.split(\"\\\\s+\").toList\n\n    words.flatMap { word =>\n      val start = text.indexOf(word, currentPos)\n      val end = start + word.length\n\n      processToken(word).map { processed =>\n        currentPos = end\n        (processed, start, end)\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Return the number of tokens in the text.\n    */\n  def countTokens(text: String): Int = {\n    tokenize(text).length\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Tokenizer for JSON structures with recursive types.\n  *\n  * @param pretty Pretty-print JSON with indentation\n  */\nclass JsonTokenizer(pretty: Boolean = false) {\n  /**\n    * Tokenize a JSON value.\n    */\n  def tokenize(value: JsonValue): Token = {\n    val jsonStr = jsonValueToString(value, pretty)\n    Token(jsonStr, STRUCTURED, Map(\"json\" -> true))\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Extract and tokenize a value at a JSON path.\n    * Path format: \"key1.key2.0.key3\" for nested access.\n    */\n  def tokenizePath(value: JsonValue, path: String): Option[Token] = {\n    val parts = path.split(\"\\\\.\")\n    traversePath(value, parts).map(tokenize)\n  }\n\n  private def traversePath(current: JsonValue, parts: Seq[String]): Option[JsonValue] = {\n    if (parts.isEmpty) return Some(current)\n\n    val (head, tail) = (parts.head, parts.tail)\n\n    current match {\n      case obj: JsonObject =>\n        obj.get(head).flatMap(traversePath(_, tail))\n\n      case arr: JsonArray =>\n        Try(head.toInt).toOption.flatMap { idx =>\n          if (idx >= 0 && idx < arr.length) {\n            traversePath(arr(idx), tail)\n          } else {\n            None\n          }\n        }\n\n      case _ => None\n    }\n  }\n\n  private def jsonValueToString(value: JsonValue, pretty: Boolean): String = {\n    value match {\n      case null => \"null\"\n      case str: String => s\"\\\"$str\\\"\"\n      case num: (Int | Long | Double | Float) => num.toString\n      case bool: Boolean => bool.toString\n      case arr: JsonArray =>\n        if (pretty) {\n          arr.map(jsonValueToString(_, pretty)).mkString(\"[\\n  \", \",\\n  \", \"\\n]\")\n        } else {\n mutable.Buffer.empty[Any]\n\n  /**\n    * Receive an item into the sink.\n    */\n  def receive(item: T): Unit = {\n    _received += item\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Drain all received items and clear the exact type matching (invariant type parameter [T]).\n  *\n  * @param default The initial value\n  * @tparam T The value type (invariant)\n  */\nclass BivariantHandler[T](default: T) {\n  private var _value: T = default\n\n  /**\n    * Get the current value.\n    */\n  def get: T = _value\n\n  /**\n    * Set a new value.\n    */\n  def set(value: T): Unit = {\n    _value = value\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Transform the value using a function.\n    */\n  def transform(func: T => T): T = {\n    _value = func(_value)\n    _value\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Abstract base tokenizer with generic input type.\n  *\n  * @tparam T The input value type\n  */\ntrait BaseTokenizer[T] {\n  /**\n    * Convert a single value to a token.\n    */\n  def tokenize(value: T): Token\n\n  /**\n    * Lazy tokenization of multiple values.\n    * Returns an iterator for memory efficiency with large batches.\n    */\n  def tokenizeBatch(values: Iterable[T]): Iterator[Token] = {\n    values.iterator.map(tokenize)\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Tokenizer for string and byte types.\n  *\n  * @param encoding The character encoding for bytes (default: UTF-8)\n  * @param normalizer Optional function to normalize strings after decoding\n  */\nclass StringTokenizer(\n    encoding: String = \"UTF-8\",\n    normalizer: String => String = identity\n) extends BaseTokenizer[Any] {\n  def tokenize(value: Any): Token = {\n    val strValue = value match {\n      case bytes: Array[Byte] =>\n        new String(bytes, encoding)\n      case bytes: Seq[Byte] =>\n        new String(bytes.toArray, encoding)\n      case str: String =>\n        str\n      case other =>\n        other.toString\n    }\n    Token(normalizer(strValue), STRING)\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Tokenizer for numeric types with precision handling.\n  *\n  * @param precision Number of decimal places for formatting (default: 6)\n  * @param formatOptions Additional formatting options\n  */\nclass NumericTokenizer(\n    precision: Int = 6,\n    formatOptions: Map[String, Any] = Map.empty\n) extends BaseTokenizer[Any] {\n  def tokenize(value: Any): Token = {\n    val strValue = value match {\n      case bd: BigDecimal =>\n        f\"${bd.toDouble}%.${precision}f\"\n      case d: Double =>\n        f\"$d%.${precision}f\"\n      case f: Float =>\n        val dval = f.toDouble\n        f\"$dval%.${precision}f\"\n      case i: Int =>\n        i.toString\n      case l: Long =>\n        l.toString\n      case other =>\n        other.toString\n    }\n    Token(strValue, NUMERIC, Map(\"original_type\" -> value.getClass.getSimpleName))\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Tokenizer for date and time types.\n  *\n  * @param formatStr Optional custom format string (ISO format or date format used if not provided)\n  */\nclass TemporalTokenizer(formatStr: Option[String] = None) extends BaseTokenizer[Any] {\n  private val isoFormatter = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern(\"yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss\")\n  private val dateFormatter = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern(\"yyyy-MM-dd\")\n\n  def tokenize(value: Any): Token = {\n    val formatter =      case _: LocalDate => dateFormatter\n          case _ => isoFormatter\n        }\n      }\n\n    val strValue = value match {\n      case ldt: LocalDateTime => ldt.format(formatter)\n      case zdt: ZonedDateTime => zdt.format(formatter)\n      case ld: LocalDate => ld.format(formatter)\n      case other => other.toString\n    }\n\n    Token(strValue, TEMPORAL)\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Universal tokenizer that handles multiple types with pattern matching dispatch.\n  * Demonstrates Scala's type-safe dispatch using sealed hierarchies.\n  */\nclass UniversalTokenizer {\n  private val stringTokenizer = new StringTokenizer()\n  private val numericTokenizer = new NumericTokenizer()\n  private val temporalTokenizer = new TemporalTokenizer()\n\n  /**\n    * Dispatch to appropriate tokenizer based on runtime type.\n    * Uses pattern matching for type-safe dispatch that replaces Python's isinstance checks.\n    */\n  def tokenize(value: Any): Token = value match {\n    case null =>\n      Token(\"NULL\", NULL)\n\n    case tokenizable: Tokenizable =>\n      Token(tokenizable.toToken, STRUCTURED)\n\n    case str: String =>\n      stringTokenizer.tokenize(str)\n\n    case bytes: case bd: BigDecimal =>\n      numericTokenizer.tokenize(bd)\n\n    case ldt: LocalDateTime | zdt: ZonedDateTime | ld: LocalDate =>\n      temporalTokenizer.tokenize(value)\n\n    case _ =>\n      // token length (longer tokens truncated)\n  * @param stripPunctuation Remove leading/trailing punctuation from tokens\n  */\nclass WhitespaceTokenizer(\n    lowercase: Boolean = false,\n    minLength: Int = 0,\n    maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n    stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n  private val punctuation = Set(\".,!?;:'\\\"()[]{}\")\n\n  /**\n    * Process a single word into a token string.\n    * Returns None if the word is filtered out.\n    */\n  private def processToken(word: String): Option[String] = {\n    var processed = word\n\n    if (stripPunctuation) {\n      processed = processed\n        .dropWhile(punctuation.contains)\n        .reverse\n        .dropWhile(punctuation.contains)\n        .reverse\n    }\n\n    if (lowercase) {\n      processed = processed.toLowerCase\n    }\n\n    if (processed.length < minLength) {\n      return None\n    }\n\n    processed = maxLength\n      .map(max => processed.take(max))\n      .getOrElse(processed)\n\n    if (processed.isEmpty) None else Some(processed)\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Split text by whitespace and return list of tokens.\n    */\n  def tokenize(text: String): List[Token] = {\n    val words = text.split(\"\\\\s+\").toList\n    words.zipWithIndex\n      .flatMap { case (word, idx) =>\n        processToken(word).map { processed =>\n          Token(\n            value = processed,\n text and return tokens with character positions.\n    * Returns tuples of (token_string, start_pos, end_pos).\n    */\n  def tokenizeWithPositions(text: String): List[(String, Int, Int)] = {\n    var JsonValue, parts: Seq[String]): Option[JsonValue] = {\n    if (parts.isEmpty) return Some(current)\n\n    val (head, tail) = (parts.head, parts.tail)\n\n    current match {\n      case obj: JsonObject    new String(bytes, encoding)\n      case bytes: Seq[Byte] =>\n        new String(bytes.toArray, encoding)\n      case str: String =>\n        str\n      case other =>\n        other.toString\n tokenize(value: Any): Token = {\n    val formatter = formatStr\n      .map(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern(_))\n      .getOrElse {\n        value match {\n          case _: LocalDateTime => isoFormatter\n */\nclass UniversalTokenizer {\n  private val stringTokenizer = new StringTokenizer()\n  private val numericTokenizer = new NumericTokenizer()\n  private val temporalTokenizer = new TemporalTokenizer()\n\n  /**\n    * Dispatch to appropriate tokenizer based on runtime type.\n    * Uses pattern matching for type-safe dispatch that replaces Python's isinstance checks.\n    */\n  def tokenize(value: Any): Token = value match {\n    case null =>\n      Token(\"NULL\", NULL)\n\n    case tokenizable: Tokenizable =>\n      Token(tokenizable.toToken, STRUCTURED)\n\n    case str: f: Float | d: Double =>\n      numericTokenizer.tokenize(f)\n\n    case bd: BigDecimal =>\n      numericTokenizer.tokenize(bd)\n\n    case ldt: LocalDateTime | zdt: ZonedDateTime | ld: LocalDate =>\n      temporalTokenizer.tokenize(value)\n\n    case _ =>\n      // Fallback: try string conversion\n      Token(value.toString, STRING, Map(\"fallback\" -> true))\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Basic tokenizer that splits text by whitespace.\n  * Fundamental operation used in NLP and text processing.\n  *\n  * @param lowercase Convert all tokens to lowercase\n  * @param minLength Minimum token length (shorter tokens filtered out)\n  * @param maxLength Optional maximum token length (longer tokens truncated)\n  * @param stripPunctuation Remove leading/trailing punctuation from tokens\n  */\nclass WhitespaceTokenizer(\n    lowercase: Boolean = false,\n    minLength: Int = 0,\n    maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n    stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n  private val punctuation = Set(\".,!?;:'\\\"()[]{}\")\n\n  /**\n    * Process a single word into a token string.\n    * Returns None if the word is filtered out.\n    */\n  private def processToken(word: String): Option[String] = {\n    var processed = word\n\n    if (stripPunctuation) {\n      processed = processed\n        .dropWhile(punctuation.contains)\n        .reverse\n        .dropWhile(punctuation.contains)\n        .reverse\n    }\n\n    if (lowercase) {\n      processed = processed.toLowerCase\n    }\n\n    if (processed.length < minLength) {\n      return None\n    }\n\n    processed = maxLength\n      .map(max => processed.take(max))\n      .getOrElse(processed)\n\n    if (processed.isEmpty) None else Some(processed)\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Split text by whitespace and return list of tokens.\n    */\n  def tokenize(text: String): List[Token] = {\n    val words = text.split(\"\\\\s+\").toList\n    words.zipWithIndex\n      .flatMap { case (word, idx) =>\n        processToken(word).map { processed =>\n          Token(\n            value = processed,\n            tokenType = STRING,\n            metadata = Map(\"position\" -> idx, \"original\" -> word)\n          )\n        }\n      }\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Split text by whitespace and return list of token strings.\n    * Convenience method returning just the string values.\n    */\n  def tokenizeToStrings(text: String): List[String] = {\n    tokenize(text).map(_.value)\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Tokenize text and return tokens with character positions.\n    * Returns tuples of (token_string, start_pos, end_pos).\n    */\n  def tokenizeWithPositions(text: String): List[(String, Int, Int)] = {\n    var currentPos = 0\n    val words = text.split(\"\\\\s+\").toList\n\n    words.flatMap { word =>\n      val start = text.indexOf(word, currentPos)\n      val end = start + word.length\n\n      processToken(word).map { processed =>\n        currentPos = end\n        (processed, start, end)\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Return the number of tokens in the text.\n    */\n  def countTokens(text: String): Int = {\n    tokenize(text).length\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Tokenizer for JSON structures with recursive types.\n  *\n  * @param pretty Pretty-print JSON with indentation\n  */\nclass JsonTokenizer(pretty: Boolean = false) {\n  /**\n    * Tokenize a JSON value.\n    */\n  def tokenize(value: JsonValue): Token = {\n    val jsonStr = jsonValueToString(value, pretty)\n    Token(jsonStr, STRUCTURED, Map(\"json\" -> true))\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Extract and tokenize a value at a JSON path.\n    * Path format: \"key1.key2.0.key3\" for nested access.\n    */\n  def tokenizePath(value: JsonValue, path: String): Option[Token] = {\n    val parts = path.split(\"\\\\.\")\n    traversePath(value, parts).map(tokenize)\n  }\n\n  private def traversePath(current: JsonValue, parts: Seq[String]): Option[JsonValue] = {\n    if (parts.isEmpty) return Some(current)\n\n    val (head, tail) = (parts.head, parts.tail)\n\n    current match {\n      case JsonObj(obj) =>\n        obj.get(head).flatMap(traversePath(_, tail))\n\n      case JsonArr(arr) =>\n        Try(head.toInt).toOption.flatMap { idx =>\n          if (idx >= 0 && idx < arr.length) {\n            traversePath(arr(idx), tail)\n          } else {\n            None\n          }\n        }\n\n      case _ => None\n    }\n  }\n\n  private def jsonValueToString(value: JsonValue, pretty: Boolean): String = {\n    value match {\n      case JsonNull => \"null\"\n      case JsonString(str) => s\"\\\"$str\\\"\"\n      case JsonNumber(num) => num.toString\n      case JsonBoolean(bool) => bool.toString\n      case JsonArr(arr) =>\n        if (pretty) {\n          arr.map(jsonValueToString(_, pretty)).mkString(\"[\\n  \", \",\\n  \", \"\\n]\")\n        } else {\n          arr.map(jsonValueToString(_, pretty)).mkString(\"[\", \",\", \"]\")\n        }\n      case JsonObj(obj) =>\n        if (pretty) {\n          obj.map { case (k, v) =>\n            s\"\\\"$k\\\": ${jsonValueToString(v, pretty)}\"\n          }.mkString(\"{\\n  \", \",\\n  \", \"\\n}\")\n        } else {\n          obj.map { case (k, v) =>\n            s\"\\\"$k\\\":${jsonValueToString(v, pretty)}\"\n          }.mkString(\"{\", \",\", \"}\")\n        }\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Sealed trait for JSON values.\n  * Replaces Python's recursive Union type with Scala algebraic data type.\n  */\nsealed trait JsonValue\n\ncase object JsonNull extends JsonValue\n\nfinal case class JsonString(value: String) extends JsonValue\n\nfinal case class JsonNumber(value: Double) extends JsonValue\n\nfinal case class JsonBoolean(value: Boolean) extends JsonValue\n\nfinal case class JsonArr(value: Vector[JsonValue]) extends JsonValue\n\nfinal case class JsonObj(value: Map[String, JsonValue]) extends JsonValue\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Registry with Nested Generics\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Registry with complex nested generic types.\n  * Demonstrates mixing mutable and immutable collections with generics.\n  *\n  * @tparam T The item type stored in containers\n  */\nclass TokenRegistry[T] {\n  private val _registry = mutable.Map.empty[String, TokenContainer[T]]\n  private val _handlers = mutable.Buffer.empty[T => Option[Token]]\n\n  /**\n    * Register a container under a key.\n    */\n  def register(key: String, container: TokenContainer[T]): Unit = {\n    _registry(key) = container\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Add a handler function.\n    */\n  def addHandler(handler: T => Option[Token]): Unit = {\n    _handlers += handler\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Process all items in a container through all handlers.\n    * Returns results from the first successful handler for each item.\n    */\n  def process(key: String): List[Option[Token]] = {\n    _registry.get(key) match {\n      case None => List()\n      case Some(container) =>\n        container.getAll.map { item =>\n          _handlers.iterator\n            .flatMap { handler =>\n              handler(item).toIterator\n            }\n            .nextOption()\n        }.toList\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Functor and Monad Abstractions\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Functor abstraction for tokens.\n  * Simulates higher-kinded types that Python cannot express.\n  *\n  * @param value The wrapped value\n  * @tparam T The value type\n  */\nclass TokenFunctor[T](val value: T) {\n  /**\n    * Map a function over the wrapped value.\n    */\n  def map[B](func: T => B): TokenFunctor[B] = {\n    new TokenFunctor(func(value))\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Flatmap (monadic bind) a function over the wrapped value.\n    */\n  def flatMap[B](func: T => TokenFunctor[B]): TokenFunctor[B] = {\n    func(value)\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Get the wrapped value or use a default if None.\n    */\n  def getOrElse(default: T): T = {\n    if (value == null) default else value\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Monad abstraction extending TokenFunctor.\n  *\n  * @param value The wrapped value\n  * @tparam T The value type\n  */\nclass TokenMonad[T](value: T) extends TokenFunctor[T](value) {\n  /**\n    * Apply a wrapped function to this monad.\n    */\n  def ap[B](funcWrapped: TokenMonad[T => B]): TokenMonad[B] = {\n    new TokenMonad(funcWrapped.value(value))\n  }\n}\n\nobject TokenMonad {\n  /**\n    * Create a monad from a pure value.\n    */\n  def pure[T](value: T): TokenMonad[T] = {\n    new TokenMonad(value)\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Builder Pattern with Fluent Interface\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Fluent builder for creating custom tokenizers.\n  * Demonstrates method chaining with generic return types in */\nclass UniversalTokenizer {\n  private val stringTokenizer = new StringTokenizer()\n  private val numericTokenizer = new NumericTokenizer()\n  private val temporalTokenizer = new .reverse\n    }\n\n    if (lowercase) {\n      processed = processed.toLowerCase\n    }\n\n    if (processed.length < minLength) {\n      return None\n    }\n\n    processed = maxLength\n text.indexOf(word, currentPos)\n      val end = start + word.length\n\n      processToken(word).map { processed =>\n        currentPos = end\n        (processed, start, end)\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Return the number of tokens in the text.\n    */\n  def countTokens(text: String): Int = {\n    tokenize(text).length\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Tokenizer for JSON structures with recursive Token = {\n    val jsonStr = jsonValueToString(value, pretty)\n    Token(jsonStr, STRUCTURED, Map(\"json\" -> true))\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Extract and tokenize a value at a JSON path.\n    * Path format: \"key1.key2.0.key3\" for nested access.\n    */\n  def tokenizePath(value: JsonValue, path: String): Option[Token] = {\n    val parts = path.split(\"\\\\.\")\n    traversePath(value, parts).map(tokenize)\n  }\n\n  private def traversePath(current: JsonValue, parts: Seq[String]): Option[JsonValue] = {\n    if (parts.isEmpty) return Some(current)\n\n    val (head, tail) = (parts.head, parts.tail)\n\n    current match {\n      case JsonObj(obj) =>\n        obj.get(head).flatMap(traversePath(_, tail))\n\n      case JsonArr(arr) =>\n        Try(head.toInt).toOption.flatMap { idx =>\n          if (idx >= 0 && idx < arr.length) {\n            traversePath(arr(idx), tail)\n          } else {\n            None\n          }\n        }\n\n s\"\\\"$str\\\"\"\n      case JsonNumber(num) => num.toString\n      case JsonBoolean(bool) => bool.toString\n      case JsonArr(arr) =>\n        if (pretty) {\n          arr.map(jsonValueToString(_, pretty)).mkString(\"[\\n  \", \",\\n  \", \"\\n]\")\n        } else {\n          arr.map(jsonValueToString(_, pretty)).mkString(\"[\", \",\", \"]\")\n        }\n      case JsonObj(obj) =>\n        if (pretty) {\n          obj.map { case (k, v) =>\n            s\"\\\"$k\\\": ${jsonValueToString(v, pretty)}\"\n          }.mkString(\"{\\n  \", \",\\n  \", \"\\n}\")\n        } else {\n          obj.map { case (k, v) =>\n            s\"\\\"$k\\\":${jsonValueToString(v, pretty)}\"\n          }.mkString(\"{\", \",\", \"}\")\n        }\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Sealed trait for JSON values.\n  * Replaces Python's recursive Union type with Scala algebraic data type.\n  */\nsealed trait JsonValue\n\ncase object JsonNull extends JsonValue\n\nfinal case class JsonString(value: String) extends Nested Generics\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Registry with complex nested generic types.\n  * Demonstrates mixing mutable and immutable _handlers = mutable.Buffer.empty[T => Option[Token]]\n\n  /**\n    * Register a container under a key.\n    */\n  def register(key: String, container: TokenContainer[T]): Unit = {\n    _registry(key) = container\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Add a handler function.\n    */\n  def addHandler(handler: T => Option[Token]): Unit = {\n    _handlers += handler\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Process all items in a container through all handlers.\n    * Returns results from the first successful handler for each item.\n    */\n  def process(key: String): List[Option[Token]] = {\n    _registry.get(key) match {\n      case None => List()\n      case Some(container) =>\n        container.getAll.map { item =>\n          _handlers.iterator\n            .flatMap { handler =>\n              handler(item).toIterator\n            }\n            .nextOption()\n        }.toList\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Functor and Monad Abstractions\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Functor abstraction for tokens.\n  * Simulates higher-kinded types that Python cannot express.\n  *\n  * @param value The wrapped value\n  * @tparam T The value type\n  */\nclass TokenFunctor[T](val value: T) {\n  /**\n    * Map a function over the wrapped value.\n    */\n  def map[B](func: T => B): TokenFunctor[B] = {\n    new TokenFunctor(func(value))\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Flatmap (monadic bind) a function over the wrapped value.\n    */\n  def flatMap[B](func: T => TokenFunctor[B]): TokenFunctor[B] = {\n    func(value)\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Get the wrapped value or use a default if None.\n    */\n  def getOrElse(default: T): T = {\n    if (value == null) default else value\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n  * Monad abstraction extending TokenFunctor.\n  *\n  * @param value The wrapped value\n  * @tparam T The value type\n  */\nclass TokenMonad[T](value: T) extends TokenFunctor[T](value) {\n  /**\n    * Apply a wrapped function to this monad.\n    */\n  def ap[B](funcWrapped: TokenMonad[T => B]): TokenMonad[B] = {\n    new TokenMonad(funcWrapped.value(value))\n  }\n}\n\nobject TokenMonad {\n  /**\n    * Create a monad from a pure value.\n    */\n  def pure[T](value: T): TokenMonad[T] = {\n    new TokenMonad(value)\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Builder Pattern with Fluent Interface\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n  * Fluent builder for creating custom tokenizers.\n  * Demonstrates method chaining with generic return types in Scala.\n  *\n  * @tparam T The input type\n  */\nclass TokenizerBuilder[T] {\n  private var _normalizers: List[String => String] = List()\n  private var _validators: List[T => Boolean] = List()\n  private var _metadata: Map[String, Any] = Map()\n\n  /**\n    * Add a normalizer function (processes string representation).\n    */\n  def withNormalizer(normalizer: String => String): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    _normalizers = _normalizers :+ normalizer\n    this\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Add a validator function (checks before tokenization).\n    */\n  def withValidator(validator: T => Boolean): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    _validators = _validators :+ validator\n    this\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Add metadata to be included with all tokens.\n    */\n  def withMetadata(metadata: (String, Any)*): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    _metadata = _metadata ++ metadata.toMap\n    this\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Build the final tokenizer function.\n    * Returns a function T => Token that applies all configured transformations.\n    */\n  def build: T => Token = {\n    val normalizers = _normalizers\n    val validators = _validators\n    val metadata = _metadata\n\n    (value: T) => {\n      // Validate\n      validators.foreach { validator =>\n        if (!validator(value)) {\n          throw new IllegalArgumentException(s\"Validation failed for $value\")\n        }\n      }\n\n      // Convert to string\n      var strValue = value.toString\n\n      // Normalize\n      strValue = normalizers.foldLeft(strValue) { (str, normalizer) =>\n        normalizer(str)\n      }\n\n      Token(strValue, STRING, metadata)\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Top-level Helper Functions\n// ============================================================================\n\nobject Tokenizers {\n  /**\n    * Helper function to tokenize a single value.\n    */\n  def toToken[T](value: T, tokenizer: BaseTokenizer[T]): Token = {\n    tokenizer.tokenize(value)\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Alias for toToken for consistency with Python.\n    */\n  def tokenize[T](value: T, tokenizer: BaseTokenizer[T]): Token = {\n    toToken(value, tokenizer)\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Tokenize a batch of values.\n    */\n  def tokenizeBatch[T](values: Seq[T], tokenizer: BaseTokenizer[T]): Seq[Token] = {\n    values.map(tokenizer.tokenize)\n  }\n\n  /**\n    * Add metadata to a token.\n    */\n  def withMetadata(token: Token, metadata: (String, Any)*): Token = {\n    token.withMetadata(metadata:
